# Co-op feature test matrix (RevNet on Deus Ex: Revision)

Goal: nothing a player can do or see in the single-player campaign is left untested in co-op. Built from the class lists of `ref/DeusEx` (1227 classes), `ref/Revision`, `ref/RevisionMission` (17 mission scripts), `ref/RevisionPawn`, `ref/RevisionDeco`, `ref/MoreTriggers`, the mode sources in `ref/sz/<Mode>`, `docs/netmode_audit.json` (636 NetMode branches) and the RevNet sources. Risk statements come from reading the code at the cited file:line (paths are relative to `ref/`; `DeusEx/` is omitted; RevNet files are `src/RevNet/Classes/*.uc`). Source snapshot: the RevNet sources of 2026-10-21 (commit ddd1ecf plus the uncommitted changes in the working tree, which include the new `Rejoined`, travel-question and crash-fix code); scenarios `Throw`, `Augs`, `Mission` and `Crawl` are credited. Nothing was run: "BUG candidate" means the code path is visibly wrong for co-op, "Unverified" means it depends on engine behaviour a live run must confirm.

## 0. Summary

### 0.1 Counts

| | P1 | P2 | P3 | Total |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| Rows | 140 | 193 | 78 | 411 |
| Covered by an existing scenario (fully or in part) | 72 | 71 | 9 | 152 |
| Untested ("none", or only the Crawl map-load smoke test) | 68 | 122 | 69 | 259 |
| of which Crawl-only | 12 | 4 | 0 | 16 |

Priority: P1 = breaks the campaign or core gameplay; P2 = visibly wrong; P3 = cosmetic. "Covered by" names an existing scenario (basic, travel, world, Talk, Progress, Save, Devices, Carry, Death, Revisit, Throw, Augs, Mission, Crawl); `Crawl-only` marks rows whose only coverage is that every campaign map loads with a client and two conversations run there; a covered row still lists in "How to test" the part that is not covered yet. Rows count features, not classes: class lists are enumerated inside the rows (59 `DeusExWeapon` classes, 41 pickups, 28 ammo, 27 augmentations, 11 skills, 17 mission scripts, 34 `MissionScript` classes, 30 `Trigger` classes + 24 `MoreTriggers`, device and mover classes, 221 pawn classes by branch, 216 carcass classes, 134 HR and 128/151 Shifter/Biomod classes).

| Area | Rows | P1 | P2 | P3 | Untested |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| Weapons, ammo and weapon mods | 59 | 14 | 35 | 10 | 50 |
| Augmentations and the energy system | 38 | 11 | 22 | 5 | 13 |
| Skills | 17 | 5 | 10 | 2 | 12 |
| Inventory items (every pickup class) and the inventory/belt system | 39 | 14 | 14 | 11 | 27 |
| NPC AI, alliances and creatures | 46 | 17 | 25 | 4 | 26 |
| Devices, doors, security and interactive map objects | 36 | 13 | 17 | 6 | 21 |
| World, physics, hazards and player movement | 34 | 8 | 13 | 13 | 23 |
| Conversations, story, mission scripts and cutscenes | 37 | 24 | 9 | 4 | 22 |
| Saves, loading, travel and map memory | 16 | 10 | 5 | 1 | 6 |
| User interface, HUD, menus and input | 28 | 11 | 11 | 6 | 15 |
| Multiplayer lifecycle, networking and administration | 20 | 7 | 11 | 2 | 11 |
| Revision-specific features | 24 | 1 | 10 | 13 | 20 |
| Other gameplay modes (Shifter, Biomod, HR, Vanilla) and their unique features | 17 | 5 | 11 | 1 | 13 |

### 0.3 Suspected real bugs found by reading the code

Confidence: High = the code path is unambiguous and RevNet does not handle it (checked by grep of `src/RevNet`); Medium/Unverified = needs a run.

| # | Area | Where | What | Confidence | Rows |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| S-01 | Revision gameplay | `WeaponRifle.uc:238-251` | `PreBeginPlay` forces `ReloadCount = 1; ReloadTime = ShotTime; bHasMuzzleFlash = True` after the `mp*` copy whenever NetMode is not Standalone. `CoopRules` only restores `mp*` defaults, so every client copy of the sniper rifle and every rifle spawned after the first tick is a one-round weapon (single-player rifle holds several rounds); client prediction reloads after each shot. | High (code read) | WPN-05 |
| S-02 | Revision gameplay | `Rocket.uc:105-115 and 160-165; RocketWP.uc:68` | Rockets of class exactly `Rocket` / `RocketWP` get `speed=2000` and `SetTimer(5)`; `Timer` calls `Explode` in a net game. Not `mp*` fields, so unpatched: GEP rockets self-detonate at 5 s and fly at a fixed speed. | High (code read) | WPN-08 |
| S-03 | Revision aug rules | `AugEMP.uc:27-31; AugAutoCounter.uc:24-28; CoopRules.uc:98-104` | MP branch sets `bAlwaysActive = False` (EMP shield and Auto-Counter are passive in the single-player rule set); `CoopRules.SinglePlayerValues` restores `LevelValues`, `EnergyRate`, `AugmentationLocation` but not `bAlwaysActive`. Every client copy, and every aug created after the first tick (travel, script grants), becomes a manual draining toggle. | High (code read) | AUG-13, AUG-20, AUG-27 |
| S-04 | Interaction | `DeusExPlayer.uc:5220-5226 (ParseRightClick), CoopPlayerCore.uc:247-263` | With a medkit selected, right-click on any non-hostile non-robot NPC (or teammate) heals it and consumes the kit instead of frobbing it, so the NPC cannot be talked to. `CoopPlayerCore.ParseRightClick` only intercepts downed players and weapon mods; the rest falls through to the MP block. | High (code read) | INV-01 |
| S-05 | Story | `RevisionMission05.uc:90-141` | The capture sequence (inventory moved to the storage room, credits confiscated, limbs raised to 50) acts on `Player` (the host) only. A remote player keeps weapons, credits and health. Same host-only pattern in M04 belt clearing (RevisionMission04.uc:496-511) and M11 knock-out/teleport (RevisionMission11.uc:75). Status: an uncommitted `CoopStory.uc` (manager 5) now does the Mission 5 capture for each remote player as it arrives (flag `RevNet_Captured`), untested; M04 and M11 are not addressed. | High (code read) | STY-11, STY-12, STY-17 |
| S-06 | Doors | `DeusExMover.uc:744-760 (DoOpen), 516 (Tick), 652 (pick time)` | In a net game any opening of a door unlocks it permanently, and every mover with its tag; locked-and-open doors are re-closed every tick; picking time becomes `pickTime / pickValue^2`. Script-opened locked doors lose their lock; lockpicking at low skill is far slower than single-player. | High (code read) | DEV-06, DEV-07, SKL-05 |
| S-07 | Hacking | `Computers.uc:144 and 176; HackableDevices.uc:235` | `Computers.Tick` clears `bLockedOut` every tick (a detected hack never locks the terminal), `ChangePlayerVisibility` returns early (NPCs keep attacking a terminal user); hack time formula `hackTime / hackValue^2` replaces the single-player constant. | High (code read) | SKL-02, SKL-08, DEV-01, DEV-02 |
| S-08 | Security devices | `SecurityCamera.uc:70-110; AutoTurretGun.uc:83-130` | Multitool on a camera or turret only disables (never re-enables), resets `hackStrength` to 0.6; the turret branch dereferences `Camera.bActive` without a None check when the turret has no linked camera view. Single-player toggles. | High (code read) | DEV-11, DEV-12 |
| S-09 | Damage model | `DeusExPlayer.uc:11005-11060 (TakeDamage MP branch), 11338-11394 (GetMPHitLocation), 3123-3165 (Landed)` | All damage to players uses the 3-region deathmatch model (head, torso+arms together, legs together) instead of six limbs; leg/torso movement penalties are Standalone only (4005). Fall damage applies two leg hits and a torso hit, and each leg hit damages both legs, so legs take two hits worth. Applies to host and clients alike. | High (code read); effect size to be measured | NPC-24, WLD-06, WLD-08 |
| S-10 | Revision props | `StaringMover.uc:419-423; AttackHelicopterPawn.uc:181` | A `StaringMover` without a `ViewTarget` (default Revision turret/eye movers) targets `GetPlayerPawn()` only, i.e. the host. Status: an uncommitted `CoopClient.Starers` now gives such movers the nearest standing player; `AttackHelicopterPawn.uc:181` is not addressed; untested. | High (code read) | REV-14 |
| S-11 | NPC AI | `ScriptedPawn.uc:6741 (HandleHacking), 6925/6930 (HandleAlarm), 1283 and 7164 (decoration instigator), 1636-1660 (FindTaggedActor), 2473 (CheckCarcassPresence)` | NPC reactions to hacking, camera/computer alarms, decoration-caused events, empty-tag orders and body suspects use `GetPlayerPawn()` (the host) as the culprit/target, whoever acted. | High (code read) | NPC-04, NPC-05, NPC-06, NPC-09 |
| S-12 | NPC AI | `ScriptedPawn.uc:690-694 (InitializePawn)` | Difficulty scaling (`SightRadius`, `HearingThreshold`, `VisibilityThreshold`, `playerCheckDistance`) is skipped when NetMode is not Standalone, i.e. for every NPC spawned after the first tick (reinforcements, MJ12 attack forces, NG+ extras, generators). | High (code read) | NPC-15, NPC-17 |
| S-13 | NPC AI | `ScriptedPawn.uc:8712-8745 and 2194-2246` | NPC scanning of players (`bCheckPlayer`) and low-priority ticks are gated on `DistanceFromPlayer` and `LastRendered()`; whether the native value is relative to the host or to the nearest player decides if NPCs near a remote player far from the host see it. | Unverified (native semantics) | NPC-02, NPC-16 |
| S-14 | Inventory UI | `PersonaScreenInventory.uc:779; PersonaScreenAugmentations.uc:866` | The inventory screen "Use" and the augmentation screen "bio-cell" button call `Activate()` on the client's own copy of the item. `Inventory.Activate` is not a replicated function (Engine Inventory.uc), and the item states are not simulated, so for a remote player the call changes nothing on the server. | Medium-high (code read; run to confirm) | INV-01, INV-02, UI-01, UI-03 |
| S-15 | Input | `DeusExPlayer.uc:7827-7860 (ActivateBelt), HUDObjectBelt.uc` | `ActivateBelt` is in the client-to-server call list, but its body needs the player's root window (`root.ActivateObjectInBelt`), which a remote pawn lacks on the host (REVNET_DEV measured). Belt hotkeys for a remote player may do nothing. | Unverified (needs a run) | INV-16, UI-14 |
| S-16 | Weapons | `DeusExWeapon.uc:5614 (DownWeapon)` | Putting a weapon away sets `ClipCount = 0` in a net game: a free full clip on every swap (single-player keeps the used clip). | High (code read) | WPN-19 |
| S-17 | Weapons | `DeusExWeapon.uc:643 and 728; sz/Rev/Engine/Weapon.uc:302` | Weapons created after the first tick get `bWeaponStay = True`; picking one up while owning the class adds ammo and then `HandlePickupQuery` returns true without destroying the pickup, so a dropped weapon can be frobbed again for more ammo. | Medium (code read; run to confirm) | WPN-18 |
| S-18 | Items | `BioelectricCell.uc:21 and 40` | Cells created after the first tick (drops, crates) and every client copy use `mpRechargeAmount` and `MaxCopies = 5`; `CoopRules` has no entry for `mpRechargeAmount`. | High (code read) | INV-02 |
| S-19 | Bodies | `DeusExCarcass.uc:585` | `ExpelInventory` returns at once in a net game: a gibbed body loses everything it carried. | High (code read) | INV-20, NPC-27 |
| S-20 | Hazards | `Cloud.uc:45 and 99; ThrownProjectile.uc:433; DeusExProjectile.uc:968` | Gas clouds hurt every pawn in radius with no line-of-sight test and appear at full size; tear gas is static; stuck darts/shuriken vanish after 5 s and recovered ones give x3. | High (code read) | WLD-03, WPN-10, WPN-14 |
| S-21 | HUD | `Revision/RevHUDHitDisplay.uc:27; TechGoggles.uc UpdateHUDDisplay; Binoculars.uc state Activated` | `RefreshGender` returns when NetMode is not Standalone so the hit display (body parts, energy, breath) is never built outside the startup window (a client always); TechGoggles/Binoculars item code dereferences `Player.rootWindow` on the server for a remote pawn. | Unverified (needs a run) | UI-06, INV-04, INV-05 |
| S-22 | Music | `Revision/RevJCDentonMale.uc:164-330` | `UpdateDynamicMusic` reads the `MusicPackageTrack` name flag from the machine's own flag base; a remote player's flags are not the host's (REVNET_DEV), so no song path may be found for a client and Revision's per-map soundtrack would be silent there. | Medium (code read; run to confirm) | REV-02 |
| S-23 | Lifecycle | `CoopGameInfo.uc:334-343 (Rejoined), 761-776 (Logout)` | New and untested: a returning player gets its things back by name from the logout record or the last save; the record is only written when a co-op save exists, so a player who leaves before the first autosave (8 s into a map) comes back fresh. `Rejoined` is in the working tree, not yet committed. | Medium (new code) | LIF-02 |
| S-24 | Power aug | `AugmentationManager.uc:514-522` | Outside Standalone the manager toggles Power Recirculator itself on any energy use (every tick check), instead of single-player's "only when it saves energy", with a sound and message per toggle. | High (code read) | AUG-03 |
| S-25 | Kill credit | `ScriptedPawn.uc:10190-10712; UNATCOTroop.uc:53; HumanCivilian.uc:109; Mission scripts with `PlayerKilled*` flags` | Everything keyed to `Killer == player` (stats, one-liners, transfer-weapon mode, `PlayerKilledSomething`, `PlayerKilledRenton`) is true only for the host; a conversation abort on NPC death (ScriptedPawn.uc:10200-10207) is also host-only. | High (code read) | WPN-32, NPC-13, STY-23 |
| S-26 | Host-only scripted effects | `FadeViewTrigger.uc:30-72; RevisionMission09.uc:538-575; RevisionMission06.uc:982; RevisionMission15.uc:108-136` | View fades, mission shakes/bobbles, proximity checks and stat checks are applied to `GetPlayerPawn()` only. Status: an uncommitted `CoopClient.Fades` forwards the host's fade to remote clients; shakes, proximity and stat checks are not addressed. | High (code read) | WLD-17, WLD-18, STY-13, STY-15, STY-20 |

### 0.4 Where the single-player code assumes one local player (not in the NetMode audit)

`GetPlayerPawn()` is the host on a listen server. Occurrences in `ref/DeusEx`: ScriptedPawn 63, DeusExWeapon 17 (2 per weapon subclass for `IncreaseStats`), DeusExProjectile 9, DeusExDecoration 9, DeusExPickup 7, AutoTurret 6, ThrownProjectile 6, SecurityCamera 4, FlagTrigger 4, DeusExCarcass 4, RiotCop 4, WaltonSimons 4, then 1-3 each in Computers, Robot, MedicalBot, RepairBot, Dog, DeusExMover, HackableDevices, LaserTrigger, BeamTrigger, AlarmUnit, MapExit, GoalCompleteTrigger, SkillAwardTrigger, NetworkTerminal and most story NPC classes. Mission scripts use `Player`/`humanPlayer` throughout. These are the root of S-05, S-10, S-11, S-13, S-25, S-26 and rows NPC-02..NPC-16, STY-*. Every new scenario should run each action once as Host and once as Remote with the other player far away.

The audit (`docs/netmode_audit.json`) covers Revision's own `DeusEx.u` only. The other modes' sources hold 587 (Shifter), 582 (Biomod), 592 (HR) and 493 (Vanilla) NetMode references in 139-170 files each; the mode-unique classes with NetMode branches are WeaponGrenade, BatonThrown, CombatKnifeThrown, Consumable, AugSkullGun, AugTracking (Shifter/Biomod) and AugRunSpeed1-4, AugCloakBase, AugRadarTransBase, AugRunSilentBase, AugSonarBase, AugSpydroneBase, AugmentationADSBase, HRAugmentationManager (HR). See GM-01..GM-08.

### 0.5 Proposed scenarios (what to build next)

Each is a `CoopTest<Name>` class in the style of the existing ones (`Run(t)`, `Remote`, `Host`, `ClientWhere`-style RPCs, `RevNetTest:` log lines).

| Scenario | Rows it carries |
|---|---|
| `Weapons` | WPN-* |
| `Augs` | AUG-* |
| `Skills` | SKL-* |
| `Items` | INV-* |
| `AI` | NPC-* |
| `Doors` | DEV-06..DEV-10, DEV-26 |
| `Gadgets` | DEV-03..DEV-14 |
| `Devices2` | DEV-02, DEV-15..DEV-20 |
| `Hazards` | WLD-01..WLD-12 |
| `World` | WLD-* |
| `Story00..Story15` | STY-* |
| `UI` | UI-* |
| `Life` | LIF-*, SAV-06, SAV-09, SAV-16, STY-36 |
| `Rev` | REV-* |
| `Modes` | GM-* |

Common harness additions that many rows need: (1) an RPC that makes Remote perform a named exec exactly as the key binding would (`ConsoleCommand` on the client) and report the server-side result; (2) a `ClientSnapshot` RPC that returns the client copy of an actor or window state (inventory list with positions, HUD child counts, `LevelValues`, `bAlwaysActive`, screen open flags) as a string; (3) a reference run on a Standalone game that records the single-player value for rows whose expected value is a number; (4) mirrored placement helpers (`PutRemote` exists) so Host and Remote get identical set-ups; (5) a switch to place Host far away (4000+ uu) while Remote acts.

### 0.6 What the existing scenarios exercise (credited in "Covered by")

| Scenario | What it does |
|---|---|
| `basic` | client joins; RPCs, movement, keypad frob, single-player rules (weapons/skills keep SP values, belt/key ring/bark manager), downed and revived both ways |
| `travel` | team travels 01 -> 02 with a flag and a medkit |
| `world` | camera alarm for a client, turret targets a client, placed grenade trap rules, dropped weapon ammo, bodies persist |
| `Talk` | conversation started by a client (continue, first choice), voices on clients, infolinks, barks to nearby players |
| `Progress` | shared notes, goals, images, nanokeys, credits, skill points, collectables, after travel |
| `Save` | checkpoint save and quick load restore flags, players' items, map state |
| `Devices` | datacube, computer login + special option, keypad, ATM, medbot heal + aug install, aug hotkey, phone |
| `Carry` | NPC/body/box positions host vs client; carry and drop bodies; lift, throw, push boxes |
| `Death` | downed, revive costs a medkit, bleed-out reverts the team to the save |
| `Revisit` | map memory: killed NPCs stay dead with bodies, moved boxes, dropped items |
| `Throw` | thrown LAM, gas, EMP, scrambler and wall LAM: positions host vs client in flight and at rest; a grenade left to go off |
| `Augs` | a remote player's augs (server-side activation): speed, cloak (characters stop seeing it, others see it translucent), ballistic, EMP shield, radar transparency, run silent, vision, light, regeneration, aggressive defense (a rocket), spy drone; the client's own view is read back |
| `Mission` | Mission 1 played by the client: talks to Paul, the commander, Alex, Manderley, Anna; story flags, goals and items logged; walks into the map exit / teleporter, answers the travel question, the team follows |
| `Crawl` | every campaign map with a client present, up to two conversations on each, the team moving map to map |

## How to read a row

- **Feature**: what a player does or sees. **Where in code**: class/function (file:line). **Co-op risk**: what differs between host and client, or can break for a remote player, with the audit/code evidence. **Covered by**: existing scenario or `none`. **How to test**: the check and what is compared host vs client.
- Common test idiom: the host spawns the situation, makes `Remote` (a client) and `Host` do the same thing, and logs `RevNetTest:` lines for server state and for the client's copy; the expected value is the single-player value read from the class defaults or the code cited in the row.

## 1. Weapons, ammo and weapon mods

> Shared facts. (a) Every `DeusExWeapon` subclass has a `PreBeginPlay` that copies `mp*` values over the single-player ones when `Level.NetMode != NM_Standalone`; that runs on the host for every weapon spawned after the first tick (NPC drops, crate contents, script spawns) and on EVERY client for its copy of every weapon (the client is always `NM_Client`). `CoopRules.PatchWeapon` only copies `mp*` class defaults back (`CoopClient.Timer` does it on a client once a weapon class is seen, after the first instance already exists); hard-coded overrides that are not `mp*` fields stay in force (see WPN-04, WPN-05, WPN-07, WPN-08). (b) A remote player's shots are generated on its client and confirmed by `ServerGenerateBullet`/`ServerHandleNotify`; the only weapon use in the existing scenarios is the thrown-grenade fire path in `Throw` (the Augs scenario spawns its rocket from script), so every row below is untested unless it says otherwise. (c) Test idiom: scenario `Weapons` gives Host and Remote the weapon and ammo, spawns a stationary hostile NPC (same class/health, e.g. UNATCOTroop 100 hp, `bInvincible=False`) in front of each at a fixed range, makes each fire a fixed number of shots through the same entry point the player's key would use (`Fire`/`AltFire` exec, ProjectileFire for projectiles), and logs `NPC.Health` delta per shot, `ClipCount`/`AmmoAmount` on the server and on the client's copy (a `ClientWhere`-style RPC), and the client's `ReloadCount/HitDamage/BaseAccuracy/AccurateRange/MaxRange` against the host's.

| ID | Feature | Where in code | Co-op risk | Pri | Covered by | How to test |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| WPN-01 | Pistol (WeaponPistol, 10mm, instant-hit) fired by a remote player | DeusExWeapon.ClientFire / DoTraceFire / ServerGenerateBullet (DeusExWeapon.uc:2422, 3000, 3478); WeaponPistol.PreBeginPlay (WeaponPistol.uc:228) | Shot is generated client-side and confirmed on the server, so a wrong client-side `ReloadCount`, `BaseAccuracy` or `ClipCount` makes the client reload/fire at different moments than the server expects. `HitDamage` etc. are not replicated: each machine computes its own, the client copy runs the MP branch of `PreBeginPlay` before `CoopClient.Timer` has patched the class defaults. Damage itself is applied by the server with the host-side (patched) values. | P1 | none | Scenario `Weapons`: Host and Remote each fire 12 shots at a fixed target 400 uu away; compare NPC health lost per shot (must equal `WeaponPistol.HitDamage` SP value, same for both), server `ClipCount` after the clip (reload after the same shot number), the client's own copy of `ReloadCount/HitDamage/BaseAccuracy` against the host's. |

## 2. Augmentations and the energy system

> Shared facts. Augmentation objects are server actors replicated to their owner (`bHasIt, bIsActive, CurrentLevel, HotKeyNum`); `Activate/Deactivate/IncLevel` are client-to-server calls (Augmentation.uc replication). Each client's copy re-runs the MP branch of the aug's `PreBeginPlay`; `CoopClient.Timer` and `CoopGameInfo.IsRelevant -> CoopRules.SinglePlayerValues` put back only `LevelValues`, `EnergyRate` and `AugmentationLocation` (CoopRules.uc:98-104), NOT `bAlwaysActive`. Energy is drained on the server in `MultiplayerTick` every 0.25 s (DeusExPlayer.uc:13782, `Role==ROLE_Authority` part); in single-player the same function runs every frame from `PlayerTick`. Test idiom: scenario `Augs` gives Host and Remote the aug at levels 1-4 (`GivePlayerAugmentation`, one aug at a time, each reset), activates it through the hotkey path the player uses (`ActivateAugByKey`/`ActivateAugmentation`), runs the effect check named in the row for 10 s, and logs `Energy` drain per second, `bIsActive`, the effect value, and the client copy's `bIsActive/CurrentLevel/LevelValues/EnergyRate/bAlwaysActive`; the expected numbers are the SP `LevelValues` and `EnergyRate` read from the class defaults.

| ID | Feature | Where in code | Co-op risk | Pri | Covered by | How to test |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| AUG-01 | Aug activation by hotkey and from the augmentation screen (ActivateAugByKey, ActivateAugmentation, ActivateAllAugs, DeactivateAll, per-aug Activate/Deactivate) | AugmentationManager.ActivateAugByKey (AugmentationManager.uc:625-660), DeusExPlayer.ActivateAugmentation (DeusExPlayer.uc:2395-2418), PersonaScreenAugmentations (PersonaScreenAugmentations.uc:840-842), CoopPlayerDevices.ClientAugDisplay | The 'new aug's hotkey' step of `Devices` covers one aug install and one activation; nothing covers toggling every aug, the HUD aug icon strip (`AddAugmentationDisplay` is rerouted to the client by CoopPlayerDevices), `bHUDShowAllAugs`, or activating via the screen (client-local `Activate` is a call to the server only for augs because it is in the aug replication list). | P1 | Augs (server-side activation of speed, cloak, ballistic, EMP, radar transparency, run silent, vision, light, regen, aggressive defense, spy drone; the client's active-aug list is read back); Devices (one new aug's hotkey) | In `Augs`: for each of the 21 campaign augs (AUG-04..AUG-24) Remote presses its hotkey, waits 1 s, presses again: server `bIsActive` true then false, client copy equal, HUD strip icon (client `augDisplay` list count) matches, 'activated' message received. |

## 3. Skills

> Shared facts. Skill objects are replicated to the owner (`CurrentLevel`); `IncLevel/DecLevel/Use` are client-to-server calls. Every skill's `PreBeginPlay` copies `mpCost1-3` and `mpLevel0-3` over `Cost[]` and `LevelValues[]` when NetMode is not Standalone (audit: Skill*.uc, `Skill.uc:51` sets `CurrentLevel=GetSkillStartLevel()`); RevNet restores them on the host next tick (`CoopGameInfo.IsRelevant`/`FixNextTick`) and on a client every second (`CoopClient.Timer`); `CoopGameInfo.GetSkillStartLevel` keeps the SP start level. Test idiom: scenario `Skills` sets the skill to level 0..3 on Remote and Host, runs the effect check, and logs the effect value; the expected value is the SP `LevelValues[level]` read from the class default.

| ID | Feature | Where in code | Co-op risk | Pri | Covered by | How to test |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| SKL-01 | Spending skill points on the skills screen (PersonaScreenSkills.UpgradeSkill, Skill.IncLevel, SkillPointsAvail, costs shown) | PersonaScreenSkills.uc:361-398, Skill.IncLevel, CoopPlayerProgress.SkillPointsAdd | The screen runs on the client and calls `selectedSkill.IncLevel()` (a client-to-server call) then reads `player.SkillPointsAvail` from the replicated value; costs displayed come from the client's `Skill.Cost[]` (MP values until `CoopClient.Timer` patches them). Points awarded by triggers are shared by CoopProgress. | P1 | Progress (skill points shared) | In `Skills`: Remote with 1000 points upgrades Lockpicking 0->3 on the screen path: server `CurrentLevel`, `SkillPointsAvail` after each step equal SP costs (e.g. 1st upgrade cost), client copy equal; cannot overspend; host's points unchanged. |

## 4. Inventory items (every pickup class) and the inventory/belt system

> Shared facts. Pickups are server actors replicated to the owner (`NumCopies`, `bActive`, `invPosX/Y`, `beltPos`). Item effects live in non-simulated state code (`state Activated`) that runs on the server; client UI code that calls `inv.Activate()` on its own copy (PersonaScreenInventory.uc:779) does nothing on the server because `Inventory.Activate` is not a replicated function (REVNET: belt hotkeys use `ActivateBelt`; the screen route is untested). MP also sets `MaxCopies=5` on medkits, lockpicks, multitools, biocells (MedKit.uc:34, Lockpick.uc:74, Multitool.uc:42, BioelectricCell.uc:21).

| ID | Feature | Where in code | Co-op risk | Pri | Covered by | How to test |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|

## 5. NPC AI, alliances and creatures

> Shared facts. NPC (`ScriptedPawn`) code is written for one local player: `GetPlayerPawn()` is used 63 times in ScriptedPawn.uc and 150+ times in the other gameplay classes. On a listen server `GetPlayerPawn()` is the HOST. Remote players are found only where the code scans the pawn list or radius actors. AI level-of-detail (`bTickVisibleOnly`, `DistanceFromPlayer`, `LastRendered()` in ScriptedPawn.Tick, ScriptedPawn.uc:8712-8730) and the enemy scan flags `bCheckPlayer/bCheckOther` (ScriptedPawn.uc:2194, 2246) depend on the distance to the engine's idea of 'the player'. NPCs created at map start get single-player difficulty scaling (they start in the Standalone window); NPCs spawned later do not (ScriptedPawn.InitializePawn, ScriptedPawn.uc:690-694: skipped when NetMode is not Standalone, leaving `SightRadius`, `HearingThreshold`, `playerCheckDistance`, `VisibilityThreshold` at Easy values). Test idiom: scenario `AI` places a stationary NPC of the class under test at 12 m with a clear line of sight to Remote and a second copy to Host (mirrored rooms or sequential runs), and logs the NPC's state (`GetStateName`, `Enemy`, `Orders`, `AgitationLevel`, `FearLevel`) every 0.5 s for 15 s, plus `AlarmTimer` and the number of NPCs alerted; Remote and Host run the identical action (stand still, walk, shoot, drop a weapon, hack, pick a lock).

| ID | Feature | Where in code | Co-op risk | Pri | Covered by | How to test |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|

Anything keyed to `GetPlayerPawn()` (turret target choice, `FindTaggedActor` with empty tag, event instigators) follows the host only. | P1 | world (turret targets client; Death: characters stop seeing a downed player) | In `AI`: UNATCO troops (friendly), MJ12 troops (hostile), civilians (neutral): Remote walks into view of each; state change must equal Host's. Fire `AllianceTrigger` (player becomes hostile to UNATCO): both players attacked. |

NPCs spawned at runtime keep Easy perception. | P1 | none | In `AI`: Host stays at the map start; Remote is moved to 4000 uu away and stands 8 m in front of a hostile troop (alert state expected within 3 s as in Host's case). Repeat with the troop spawned at runtime by `Spawn` (compare `SightRadius`, `HearingThreshold`, `VisibilityThreshold` with an NPC placed in the map). |

## 6. Devices, doors, security and interactive map objects

> Shared facts. A device's screen opens on the user's own machine on its copy of the device; what it changes in the world goes to the host with `Server*` calls (CoopPlayerDevices). A remote pawn has no root window on the host, so any server-side device code that reaches into `rootWindow` fails silently for remote users. MP rules that affect every device: hackable devices re-arm after `TimeToReset` (set huge by `CoopGameInfo.IsRelevant` for objects created after the first tick; map-placed ones are created in the Standalone window and keep their SP value), camera and turret `bInvincible` and `hackStrength` are set only in the startup window (so SP stays), `HackableDevices.Frob` hack time uses `hackTime / hackValue^2`, `Computers.Tick` clears `bLockedOut`. Test idiom: scenario `Doors`/`Gadgets`/`Devices2` puts Host and Remote in front of the device (`Remote.SetLocation` + `ClientSetLocation`), has each perform the identical frob (`DoFrob` with the held tool) and logs device state on server and on the client's copy plus the player's inventory/credits/flags.

| ID | Feature | Where in code | Co-op risk | Pri | Covered by | How to test |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|

MP behaviour for the host: a terminal with no user accounts skips the login screen and starts the hack game; `state On.Tick` closes a terminal whose user is >1500 uu away (`curFrobber` rule); terminal user is not made invisible to NPCs (Computers.uc:176). ## 7. World, physics, hazards and player movement

> Shared facts. World effects are host-side; players only see replicated actors and effects. Damage to players goes through the 3-region MP model (NPC-24), so every hazard row also compares limb results. Test idiom: scenario `Hazards`/`World` puts Host and Remote in a mirrored hazard (or the same hazard in sequence), applies it for a fixed time, and logs health per limb, `Energy`, status flags (`bOnFire`, `drugEffectTimer`, poison), and the client's view of the hazard actors (count and `Location` of the Fire/Cloud/Fragment actors).

| ID | Feature | Where in code | Co-op risk | Pri | Covered by | How to test |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|

## 8. Conversations, story, mission scripts and cutscenes

> Shared facts. Mission scripts (`RevisionMissionNN`, subclasses of `RevMissionScript`/`MissionScript`) run once on the host with `player` = the host's pawn (`GetPlayerPawn()`) and the host's `flagBase`. Remote players have their own, mostly empty `flagBase` (REVNET_DEV); RevNet copies trigger flags and shares goals/notes/images/keys/credits/skill points from the host. Each row lists the scripted events of the mission, which of them are written for one player (`Player.*`, `humanPlayer.*`, `player.Location`, belt/inventory edits through the host's root window) and what the co-op test must show. The existing `Mission` scenario (Mission 1 walkthrough: client talks to Paul, the commander, Alex, Manderley and Anna; story flags, goals and items; travel through the map-exit and teleporter confirmation popups) is credited to the 01 rows only. Test idiom: scenario `Story<NN>` loads the mission map with the story flags a real run would have, has Remote perform the scripted action (talk, enter trigger, kill, give item) with Host elsewhere, and compares flags/goals/notes/inventory of both players and the world result (spawned/hidden NPCs, door states).

| ID | Feature | Where in code | Co-op risk | Pri | Covered by | How to test |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| STY-01 | Conversations started by frob, by proximity (ConversationTrigger) and by script; choices, flag checks, flag sets, item/credit/goal/note/skill-point events, multi-speaker scenes, camera moves | CoopPlayerTalk.StartConversation (CoopPlayerTalk.uc:61-120), CoopConPlay (CoopConPlay.uc:62-254), CoopConClient, ConversationTrigger.uc:20-61 (`player = Instigator`), ConEvent* (Add Credits, AddGoal, AddNote, AddSkillPoints, Animation, CheckFlag, CheckObject, CheckPersona, Choice, Jump, MoveCamera, RandomLabel, SetFlag, Speech, Trade, TransferObject, Trigger) | Host runs the conversation for a remote player with the host's flags; not run through CoopConPlay: `ConEventTrade` (vendor windows), `ConEventCheckPersona` (not overridden by CoopConPlay: check it reads the speaking remote pawn's skills/augs/credits), `ConEventTransferObject` (item between player and NPC), `ConEventAnimation` on the player. | P1 | Talk (client conversation, choices, voices); Mission (Mission 1 story characters); Crawl (up to two conversations per map) | In `Talk`: run one conversation per event type with Remote (a recorded `.con` that uses it, or a synthetic `Conversation`): item given appears in Remote's inventory only, credits to Remote, goal/note/skill points shared, trade window opens on Remote (vendor map 04/06), persona check uses Remote's skills. |

## 9. Saves, loading, travel and map memory

> Shared facts. A co-op save is a checkpoint (CoopSave: host flags, goals, notes, keys, images, each player's core stats/skills/augs/inventory by name; CoopMapMemory: gone/moved/dropped things, bodies, doors per map); maps always reload fresh from file. Native DeusEx saves are not used. Existing scenarios: Save, Travel, Revisit, Death, Progress.

| ID | Feature | Where in code | Co-op risk | Pri | Covered by | How to test |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| SAV-01 | Autosave on map entry (8 s after start) and host quick-save (QuickSave, SaveRule 0/1) | CoopGameInfo.Tick/SaveCoopGame (CoopGameInfo.uc:173-176, 590-613), CoopPlayerCore.QuickSave (CoopPlayerCore.uc:86-97) | Autosave fires after the mission script's first frame; a game state that is mid-event (conversation, cutscene) at that moment is saved as is. Every MissionScript/Autosave actor autosave is disabled in net games. | P1 | Save (checkpoint save/quick load) | Extend `Save`: autosave while Remote is in a conversation and while an NPC fight is on; quick-save in each state (swimming, carrying a box, on fire, downed); `SaveRule=1` refuses quick save with message. |
| SAV-02 | Quick-load / continue / load from the menu (LoadCoopGame, LoadGame exec, RestoreFromSave, ?CoopLoad, by way of the menu map) | CoopGameInfo.LoadCoopGame/RestoreFromSave (CoopGameInfo.uc:649-758), CoopPlayerCore.QuickLoad/LoadGame (CoopPlayerCore.uc:99-113) | Clients are told to rejoin (CoopLaunch.Rejoin, 15 tries); a returning player is matched to the saved record by name at Login (CoopGameInfo.uc:254-265) and restored in `PostLogin`; a player who reconnects after the others, or under another name, needs checking. | P1 | Save, Death (bleed-out reverts team) | Add: quick-load with 3 players where one reconnects 20 s late: late player gets their saved items by name; a new name joining after a load gets a fresh character; load when the saved map equals the current map. |
| SAV-03 | What each player record keeps (health per limb, energy, credits, skill points, skills, augs with level and hotkey, weapons with mods/clip/ammo type/laser/scope/silencer, ammo, stackables with copies and charge, belt positions) and what it drops (active aug state, ammo loaded type per weapon of NPC, inventory grid positions, equipped item, drug/poison/fire status, `bCloaked`, kill counters, stats, picked-up key ring, text of notes by player) | CoopSave.SavePlayer/LoadPlayer (CoopSave.uc:409-600) | Lines `C,S,A,M,P,W` only; unknown item classes are logged and skipped ('save skips item'); `MaxLines=1200` and `MaxPlayers=16` limits. | P1 | Save (items restored) | In `Save`: Remote with 40 mixed items (all weapon classes with each mod, every pickup class, 5 augs) saved/loaded: diff of item list (class, NumCopies, Charge, ClipCount, mods, beltPos, invPosX/Y) before/after must be empty, or the difference is documented; save with more than 1200 lines does not corrupt the file. |

| SAV-06 | Travel timing: host loads while clients follow (port closed, '?failed' rejoin), slow clients, a client that fails to follow | CoopLaunch (CoopLaunch.uc), REVNET_DEV 'While the host loads the next map...' | Clients retry 15 times; a client that exceeds the tries sits on the menu. | P1 | travel | In `Life`: delay one client's reconnect by 30 s during a travel; it must land on the new map with its character; a client that quits during travel does not block the team. |
| SAV-07 | Return to an earlier map (backtracking: HQ <-> Island, NYC streets between missions, Paris maps) with the mission script's changes (items stored, NPCs hidden/unhidden) | CoopMapMemory, mission scripts (STY rows) | Fresh map + script flags + map memory; combination order matters (the mission script's FirstFrame runs on the first timer and the autosave is scheduled 8 s after start). | P1 | Revisit | In `Revisit`: 01 -> HQ -> 01 -> HQ -> 02 -> 01 (backtrack through a mission change): nothing duplicated (items stored once), nothing resurrected. |

| SAV-11 | Save while specific states are active: conversation, infolink, computer screen, medbot screen, cutscene, carried body/box, charged item active, burning, poisoned, underwater | CoopGameInfo.SaveCoopGame (CoopGameInfo.uc:622-645) refuses only `Host.IsInState('Dying')` | State at save time is partly recorded (inventory, flags); transient states are lost; the host's conversation flag (`ConvInProgress`) could be saved mid-way. | P2 | none | In `Save`: save during each state, load: no stuck conversation flags, no frozen NPC, charged item reset. |

## 10. User interface, HUD, menus and input

> Shared facts. UI code runs on each machine with that machine's NetMode (host `NM_ListenServer`, client `NM_Client`), so every `Level.NetMode != NM_Standalone` branch in a window class is taken on both. A remote pawn has no root window on the host. Persona screens are enabled by `bBeltIsMPInventory=False`. Compare by logging the window's state in the client via `ClientTest`-style commands (`ClientDeviceTest` pattern) and screenshots if the harness can take them (the live runner mutes and minimizes windows).

| ID | Feature | Where in code | Co-op risk | Pri | Covered by | How to test |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| UI-01 | Inventory screen (PersonaScreenInventory/RevPersonaScreenInventory: item list, drag/drop, use, drop, equip, ammo selection, item info, nanokey ring window, credits, belt strip, 'inventory full') | PersonaScreenInventory.uc:282-1935 (incl. UseSelectedItem 779), RevPersonaScreenInventory.uc, PersonaInventoryObjectBelt.uc:23, DeusExRootWindow.ProcessDataVaultSelection (DeusExRootWindow.uc:264), PersonaScreenBaseWindow.uc:108 | BUG candidate: 'use' calls `inv.Activate()` on the client's copy (not replicated); drop uses `DropItem` (call to server); belt width 631 vs enlarged belt SP only; outside Standalone the hotkey cannot open another screen from inside a screen; Ctrl+Tab cycling is SP only. | P1 | none | In `UI`: Remote opens the screen (key bound or `ShowInventoryWindow`): all items listed with correct icons/counts; use a medkit and a biocell via 'Use': server `Health`/`Energy` rise; equip a weapon by double click; drop with the Drop button; close with Esc and the key. |

## 11. Multiplayer lifecycle, networking and administration

| ID | Feature | Where in code | Co-op risk | Pri | Covered by | How to test |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| LIF-01 | Join mid-mission (new player joins an in-progress map) | CoopGameInfo.Login/PostLogin (CoopGameInfo.uc:241-343), CoopProgress.PlayerJoined (CoopProgress.uc:277-318), CoopTalk/CoopDevices.PlayerJoined | New player spawns on the map start spot and steps aside; gets the host's goals/notes/images/keys; skills/augs/items are the starting state; flags the host already set (`PlayerTraveling`) are cleared for it. Not solved: items already granted to the team before the join. | P1 | basic, travel, Progress (joins and gets lists) | In `Life`: Remote joins 5 min into 02 after Host has 3 skills upgraded: Remote starts as a fresh JC (documented); Remote receives the current goals, notes, images, keys; no duplicate mission-script items; Host unaffected; third player joins later. |
| LIF-02 | Disconnect and rejoin mid-mission (same name) | CoopGameInfo.Logout (CoopGameInfo.uc:761-776), CoopGameInfo.PostLogin restore (only when `bRestore`) | New code, untested: `Rejoined` (CoopGameInfo.uc:334-343) gives a returning player what it had when it left or in the last save, by name, unless it arrives with the team (`PlayerTraveling`) or on a co-op load; the logout record is written only when a co-op save exists (`SavedMap!=""`, CoopGameInfo.Logout), so a player who leaves before the first autosave comes back fresh. | P1 | none | In `Life`: Remote with 10 items quits (client `exit`), rejoins after 30 s (before and after the first autosave): restored by name; log inventory/skills/augs on both ends; no ghost pawn left (count of PlayerPawns), no stuck conversation/computer/lockpick (curFrobber/pickPlayer cleared). |
| LIF-03 | Disconnect cleanup (conversation in progress, computer open, carrying a box/body, picking a lock, spy drone active, grenades owned, downed state) | CoopConPlay.PlayerLeft (CoopConPlay.uc:104), CoopPlayerTalk.Destroyed (CoopPlayerTalk.uc:204), Computers.curFrobber, DeusExMover.pickPlayer, ThrownProjectile owner logged out (ThrownProjectile.uc:67) | Owned grenades become disabled when their owner leaves; open devices keep references to the gone pawn. | P1 | none | In `Life`: Remote quits during each state; Host continues: conversation NPC returns to its orders, terminal usable again, door pickable, thrown mines disabled. |

## 12. Revision-specific features

> Shared facts. Revision's own classes (package `Revision`, `RevisionMission`, `RevisionDeco`, `RevisionPawn`, `RevisionUI`) are subclasses of the DeusEx ones and add features that assume one local player: `RevJCDentonMale` (4113 lines), `RevGameInfo.ApplyGamemode` (1760 lines, applies all gameplay modifiers once per map from the host's settings, CoopGameInfo.uc:416-436), `RevChallengeManager` (1621 lines), `RevMissionScript` (14895 lines, 56 challenge setups). Audit coverage of Revision-only classes is thin (RevJCDentonMale 16 entries, RevGameInfo 4, RevScopeView 3, RevMutatorCustomDiff* 33).

| ID | Feature | Where in code | Co-op risk | Pri | Covered by | How to test |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| REV-01 | Weather: rain, snow, hail, lightning, sandstorms (Precipitator, PrecipitationZone/Node/Shelter, Raindrop, Snowflake, Hailstone, RevLightningZone, TheWeatherPan/MeteorShower cheats, RevMenuChoice_Weather) | CoopClient.Tick (CoopClient.uc:41-95), Precipitation/*.uc, Revision/RevRaindrop.uc, RevLightningZone.uc | Documented: clients get no weather (ponytail comment: 'set it up on clients too if that is missed'); host precipitation actors' `RemoteRole` forced None; lightning flashes/thunder in `RevLightningZone` are zone-based. | P3 | none | In `Rev`: Hong Kong/Paris rain map with Host and Remote: host precipitator count > 0, client 0, network actor count stable over 60 s, no log errors; lightning flash seen (ambient light) by both or documented absent on client. |
| REV-02 | Dynamic music: Revision soundtrack (DXOgg: DXOggMusicManager/DXOggMusicTrigger/DXOggMusicInfo), tracker fallback, ambient/combat/conversation/death/outro states, per-map music packages and `MusicPackageTrack` flag | RevJCDentonMale.UpdateDynamicMusic (RevJCDentonMale.uc:164-497), RevGameInfo.SetupMusic (RevGameInfo.uc:218-290), RevisionMission*.SetMusicPackage, DXOgg/*.uc | The player's tick on each machine reads `FlagBase.GetName('MusicPackageTrack')` from the machine's own `flagBase` (a remote player's flags are not the host's): no song path => no music for the client. Combat detection scans pawns near the local player (server-only states such as `Attacking` are not replicated) so combat music may never start on clients. | P2 | none | In `Rev`: on Remote's client log `Song`, `musicModeRev`, current OGG track; enter combat (NPC attacking Remote) and a conversation: music mode changes ambient -> combat -> conversation -> ambient within SP timings (musicCheckTimer); compare with Host. |
| REV-03 | Collectables (DXCollectable, RevHUDCollectableDisplay, CollectablesMode, bonuses: energy, damage reduction, skill points) | Revision/DXCollectable.uc, CoopProgress.Collectable (CoopProgress.uc:321-366) | Counted for the team in collectables mode with per-player bonuses (skill points to finder, shared). | P2 | Progress (collectables) | Extend: counter equals on both HUDs after find #1, #2, #3; energy bonus on #1/#4 for both, reduction on #3; map flag `<MAP>_CF` set once. |
| REV-04 | Challenges (RevChallengeManager, RevChallengeMenu, 56 `DoChallengeNFirstFrame/Timer` setups in RevMissionScript, chlng_* modes, bests) | Revision/RevChallengeManager.uc, RevisionMission/RevMissionScript.uc:1980-14900, ScriptedPawn.Died challenge branches (ScriptedPawn.uc:10200-10280) | Challenge state lives in the host's pawn and on `RevChallengeManager` config; counters on the host's HUD (`countWin`); reachable only from the main menu challenge screen (greyed in net games) or by cheat. | P3 | none | In `Rev`: confirm challenge menus are disabled in co-op and that a challenge map loaded by `Goto` does not corrupt the co-op game (document). |
| REV-05 | Achievements and Steam stats (SetAchievement, RevAchievementsMenu, RevGlobalStatsMenu, RevStatsMenu, ACH_* triggers, AchievementTrigger) | RevJCDentonMale.SetAchievement (RevJCDentonMale.uc:1793), Revision/AchievementTrigger.uc, mission scripts | No achievement is granted in a net game; counters in `Human` still increase for the host or the killer where the host-only checks pass. | P3 | none | In `Rev`: log whether `SetAchievement` returns early for host and Remote; no error spam. |
| REV-06 | HDTP models and textures (Facelift: weapons, decorations, NPCs, carcasses, pickups, player; HDTP settings; GlobalFacelift; ServerPackages HDTP) | Human.GlobalFacelift/GetHDTPSettings (Human.uc:1446, 2201), DeusExDecoration.PreBeginPlay (DeusExDecoration.uc:110), ScriptedPawn.PreBeginPlay (ScriptedPawn.uc:542), RevGameInfo.ApplyGamemode (RevGameInfo.uc:511-513), REVNET_DEV (package map) | In a net game `Facelift` is skipped, so NPCs/weapons/props stay vanilla-looking; things spawned in the Standalone window may still load HDTP meshes; a client without the HDTP package gets no mesh (fixed through ServerPackages); mixed states between actors of the same class. | P2 | Carry (mesh compared host vs client) | In `Rev`: compare `Mesh`/`Skin` of 30 sample actors (weapons, NPCs, crates, pickups) on host vs client; no 'none' meshes; client with and without HDTP installed. |

Options that are Standalone-only in code are inert: enlarged belt, item refusal, hawk heavy ammo, prod/pistol-only (DeusExPlayer.uc:6089/6102), custom energy recharge (1826), camera memory/see bodies (SecurityCamera.uc:257/353), HDTP. | P2 | none | In `Rev`: for each of 12 representative options (Zombie, Ninja, OneOfUs, CloakedEnemies, QuietEnemies, TeleportingPawns, InstantCameras, SmallItems, OneItemInventory, NoSpareAmmo, TransferWeapon, SkipCutscenes) start a co-op game with it on: world effect present, remote player rule applies as documented (host-only vs all), no script errors. |

## 13. Other gameplay modes (Shifter, Biomod, HR, Vanilla) and their unique features

> Shared facts. A mode replaces only `DeusEx.u` and `RevisionMission.u`; one `RevNet.u` serves all five (basic, Talk and Save were verified on each). `netmode_audit.json` covers only Revision's own DeusEx.u: the other modes' DeusEx sources contain 493 (Vanilla), 587 (Shifter), 582 (Biomod), 592 (HR) NetMode references in 139-170 files each (counted in ref/sz) with no audit. Rows below are the mode-unique parts; every row above must also be re-run per mode (see GM-01).

| ID | Feature | Where in code | Co-op risk | Pri | Covered by | How to test |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| GM-01 | Whole matrix under each mode (Vanilla, Shifter, Biomod, HR) | tools/net_modes.sh, tools/check_modes.py, REVNET_DEV 'Gameplay modes' | Property/override differences (5 LevelValues vs 4, `AddNote` signature, `StartConversationByName` extra argument, `HealPlayer` signature); a signature mismatch fails only in that mode. | P1 | basic, Talk, Save under all five modes | Run `Weapons`, `Augs`, `Skills`, `Items`, `Doors`, `Mission` (01) in each mode: pass/fail table by mode. |
| GM-02 | Shifter weapons: WeaponBlackjack, WeaponBoomstick, WeaponGrenade, WeaponJackHammer, WeaponLoBruto, WeaponMagnum, WeaponMiniRocket, WeaponModAuto, WeaponPrecisionRifle, WeaponPrototypeSword(A,B,C), WeaponRailgun, WeaponToxinBlade, thrown BatonThrown/CombatKnifeThrown/CrowbarThrown/FreedomThrown, ammo (Ammo10mmEX, AmmoCombatKnife, AmmoDragon, AmmoPS20), projectiles (FireballDragon, FireComet2, NapalmBomb, PlasmaBolt2, RocketMini2) | sz/Shifter/DeusEx (128 new classes; NetMode in WeaponGrenade:4, BatonThrown:1, CombatKnifeThrown:1) | New classes have their own MP branches that CoopRules (which patches only `mp*` names present on all classes) may not cover; thrown melee weapons spawn projectiles under non-standalone rules. | P2 | none | In `Modes` (Shifter): give Remote each; fire/throw; damage per hit vs class default, pickup of thrown melee, ammo counts. |

## 14. The Mech class (docs/MECH_CLASS.md)

> Shared facts. A player picks the class in the launcher ("You play as"): `[RevNet.CoopLaunch] Agent=Mech` in the user ini; `CoopLaunch.Take` adds `?RevClass=Mech` to every URL the game opens (so a rejoin or a follow after travel keeps it); `CoopGameInfo.PlayerClass` spawns `CoopMechPlayer`. Test idiom: `tools/run_net_live.ps1 -ClientAgent Mech` (and/or `-HostAgent Mech`); `CoopTestMech` compares the Mech client with a nano host.

| ID | Feature | Where in code | Co-op risk | Pri | Covered by | How to test |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| MECH-01 | Class choice reaches the game (client and host), kept over travel and rejoins | CoopLaunch.Take, CoopGameInfo.PlayerClass / ApproveClass, relay `set_auto_open` | A URL without `?RevClass` (a rejoin by `host:port`) would make a JC; `RevGameInfo.Login` swaps a class `ApproveClass` refuses. | P1 | Mech, travel (`-ClientAgent Mech`, `-HostAgent Mech`) | Class on 01 and after travel to 02 (client and host); class after `reconnect`. Measured 2026-10-24: all `CoopMechPlayer`. |
| MECH-02 | Body: Commando mesh, robot-plating skins, collision and mesh height | CoopMechPlayer.MechLook / Facelift / SetSkin / TravelPostAccept | Revision's look code (HDTP, costumes, JC's face in skin 0) runs on the host and the player's machine; a taller mesh sinks into the floor; a wider collision would block vents. | P2 | Mech, Look (screenshots) | Mesh and skins on the host; PrePivot 4.65 (JC 2.25); collision 20x43 like JC's; `CoopTestLook` screenshots from the host. |
| MECH-03 | Damage plating, no fall damage, EMP weakness, less knockback | CoopMechPlayer.TakeDamage / DamageMult | Damage is the host's; difficulty multiplies both players alike. | P1 | Mech | Same hits on Mech and host: shot 20 -> 28 vs 40 torso; fall 30 -> 0 vs 30 legs; EMP 10 -> 10 torso (an EMP's energy drain comes over the next seconds: DeusExPlayer.EnergyDrain). |
| MECH-04 | Heavier and slower: speed x0.88, jump 270 (JC 300), mass 270, loud low footsteps | CoopMechPlayer.GetCurrentGroundSpeed, defaults, PlayFootStep | Revision derives the jump from the class default (SkillManager: Swimming); a runtime change was reset by `ResetPlayerToDefaults`. | P2 | Mech | Speed 268 vs 304, jump 270 vs 300, mass 270 vs 150 (host values; JumpZ replicates to the owner). Footstep loudness to characters: none. |
| MECH-05 | Built-in arms: Arm Blades and Arm Cannon given at every login, on belt 1 and 2, never dropped | CoopMechPlayer.GiveArsenal / DropItem, CoopMechBlade, CoopMechCannon (DropFrom) | Given before a rejoin's restore, they were replaced (stale belt slot); a nano player loading a Mech record must not get them. | P1 | Mech | Arms and belt slots on join; `DropItem(cannon)` false and still owned; after reconnect: both, slots 1 and 2 (client's own belt too). Nano loading a Mech record: none. |
| MECH-06 | Arm Cannon fires through the client's key (automatic, 7.62 mm), blades swing | CoopMechCannon defaults (WeaponMiniCrossbow), CoopMechBlade (WeaponCombatKnife) | The client's copy predicts with its own values (`ServerWeaponValues`). | P1 | Mech | Client's copy: clip 0/40, damage 5; after `fire`: clip 10, ammo 40 -> 30 on the host; blades: client state `ClientFiring`, anim `Attack`. Damage to a character: none yet. |
| MECH-07 | Nano canisters can't be installed | CoopMechPlayer.ServerInstallAug, CoopMedAugs.InstallAugmentation | The client's screen would show the canister installable. | P2 | none | Mech at a medical bot with a canister: the message, the canister stays, no aug. |
| MECH-08 | Restored weapons keep their belt slots (all players) | CoopSave.LoadPlayer (belt flags put back) | The first weapon put on the belt came back with its slot holding it but `bInObjectBelt` cleared (found with the Mech; a pistol too). | P2 | Mech | Before leaving vs after reconnect: every weapon's `beltPos` and the client's belt. |
| MECH-09 | First-person arms: metal hands, crossbow model for the cannon, knife model for the blades | CoopMechCannon/CoopMechBlade.GetWeaponHandTex | Hand textures are chosen on the machine that draws (the client). | P3 | Look (host only) | Screenshot on a client (needs a visible client window). |

| MECH-11 | Salvage from a destroyed robot, once, to every Mech near it | CoopWorld.Killed / Salvaged, CoopMechPlayer.RobotSalvage | A robot's death is told twice. | P2 | MechTree | Mini spider bot destroyed beside the Mech: +3. Turrets and cameras: none (not built). |
| MECH-12 | Buying and raising: salvage, prerequisites, the host's check | CoopMechPlayer.ServerUpgradeModule, CoopMechAug.BlockedFor / CostFor | A client could ask for a locked or unaffordable module. | P1 | MechTree | Shrapnel Burst refused before Arm Blades 2, then bought; costs taken; maxed module "N/A". |

## 15. The assault rifle as a 7.62 battle rifle (host setting RifleRework)

| ID | Feature | Code | Network risk | Pri | Covered by | Measured |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|

| RIFLE-04 | Select fire on the ammo key: full auto, semi-automatic (one round a pull), 20mm, full auto | CoopPlayerCore.SwitchAmmo / ServerSelectFire / Fire | A weapon fires again while the fire button is down (Button bFire). | P1 | Rifle | Fire held 2 s: full auto 16 rounds, semi 1; 20mm then 7.62 full auto. |
| RIFLE-05 | The Mech's arm cannon keeps step (level 1 = a rifle round) | CoopMechPlayer.RefreshModules | | P3 | none | Not measured live (cannon 18 / 22 / 25 / 32 by the formula). |
| RIFLE-06 | The launcher's "Assault rifle" setting | revcoop_relay.c apply_settings (RifleRework) | Host only: clients get the values from the host. | P2 | tools/run_launcher_test.ps1 -Rifle 0/1 | Through the launcher's window: "The mode's own" -> settings.txt Rifle=1, RevNet.ini RifleRework=0, the host's co-op game without the rework; "7.62 battle rifle" -> RifleRework=1, rifle 4 -> 18, 0.1 -> 0.15 s, recoil 0.5 -> 0.75. PASS both. |

## Appendix A. Class inventory: every class of the families named in the task and the row that tests it

Generated from the class lists (`ref/DeusEx`, `ref/Revision`, `ref/RevisionPawn`, `ref/RevisionDeco`, `ref/RevisionMission`; `(Rev)` marks Revision-package classes). A class is mapped to the first row that names it; otherwise to the family sweep row shown in brackets with `*`. A pawn or carcass class has no logic of its own beyond the branch it extends, so the sweep rows (NPC-18..NPC-23, NPC-27) test it by spawning one instance of each.
